\chapter*{导数专题练习}
\begin{enumerate}
    \item 瞬时速度、平均速度
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 若质点 $A$ 运动的位移 $S$ (单位: ${m}$ )与时间 $t$ (单位: ${s}$ )之间的函数关系是 $S\left( t\right)  =  - \dfrac{2}{t}\left( {t \geq  1}\right)$ ,那么该质点在 $t = 3{s}$ 时的瞬时速度和从 $t = 1{s}$ 到 $t = 3{s}$ 这两秒内的平均速度分别为\blkda{$\dfrac{2}{9},\dfrac{2}{3}$}

        \begin{jieda}
            $\dfrac{\Delta S}{\Delta t} = \dfrac{S\left( {3 + {\Delta t}}\right)  - S\left( 3\right) }{\Delta t} = \dfrac{\dfrac{2}{3 + {\Delta t}} + \dfrac{2}{3}}{\Delta t} = \dfrac{2}{3\left( {3 + {\Delta t}}\right) }$ ,
            
            所以 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta t} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{\Delta S}{\Delta t} = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta t} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{2}{3\left( {3 + {\Delta t}}\right) } = \dfrac{2}{9}$ 即该质点在 $t = {3s}$ 时的瞬时速度为 $\dfrac{2}{9}$ ;
            
            从 $t = 1{s}$ 到 $t = 3{s}$ 这两秒内的平均速度为 $\dfrac{S\left( 3\right)  - S\left( 1\right) }{3 - 1} = \dfrac{-\dfrac{2}{3} + 2}{2} = \dfrac{2}{3}$
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 某物体的运动方程为 $s\left( t\right)  = 4{t}^{2} + 2$ (位移单位:m,时间单位:s), \\若 $v = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta t} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{s\left( {3 + {\Delta t}}\right)  - s\left( 3\right) }{\Delta t} = {24}{m}/{s}$ ,则下列说法中正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        \xx{${24}{m}/{s}$ 是物体从开始到 $3{s}$ 这段时间内的平均速度}{${24}{m}/{s}$ 是物体从 $3{s}$ 到 $\left( {3 + {\Delta t}}\right) {s}$ 这段时间内的速度}{${24}{m}/{s}$ 是物体在 $3{s}$ 这一时刻的瞬时速度}{${24}{m}/{s}$ 是物体从 $3{s}$ 到 $\left( {3 + {\Delta t}}\right) {s}$ 这段时间内的平均速度}
        \begin{jieda}
            由 $v = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta t} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{s\left( {3 + {\Delta t}}\right)  - s\left( 3\right) }{\Delta t} = {24}{m}/{s}$，可知，$24m/s$ 是物体在 ${{3s}}$ 这一时刻的瞬时速度.
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 如果说某物体做直线运动的时间与距离满足 $s\left( t\right)  = 2{\left( 1 - t\right) }^{2}$ , 则其在 $t = {0.5}$ 时的瞬时速度为\blkda{$-2$}

        \begin{jieda}
            根据导数的定义可得,在 $t = {0.5}$ 时的瞬时速度为${s}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta t} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{s\left( {{0.5} + {\Delta t}}\right)  - s\left( {0.5}\right) }{\Delta t} = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta t} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{2{\left( 1 - {0.5} - \Delta t\right) }^{2} - 2{\left( 1 - {0.5}\right) }^{2}}{\Delta t} =  - 2$
        \end{jieda}
    \end{enumerate}
    \item 平均变化率、瞬时变化率
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 若函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - x$ ,则函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 从 $x = 1$ 到 $x = 3$ 的平均变化率为\blkda{3}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - x$ ,所以 $f\left( 1\right)  = {1}^{2} - 1 = 0,f\left( 3\right)  = {3}^{2} - 3 = 6$，故函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 从 $x = 1$ 到 $x = 3$ 的平均变化率为 $\dfrac{\Delta y}{\Delta x} = \dfrac{f\left( 3\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{3 - 1} = \dfrac{6 - 0}{2} = 3$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2}$ 在 $x = 2$ 处的瞬时变化率为\blkda{4}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $\dfrac{f\left( {2 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 2\right) }{\Delta x} = \dfrac{{\left( 2 + \Delta x\right) }^{2} - 4}{\Delta x} = \dfrac{4 + {4\Delta x} + {\left( \Delta x\right) }^{2} - 4}{\Delta x} = {\Delta x} \neq  2$，所以,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2}$ 在 $x = 2$ 处的瞬时变化率为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {2 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 2\right) }{\Delta x}$
            
            $= \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\left( {{\Delta x} + 4}\right)  = 4.$
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 下列函数中，在区间$[-1,0]$上的平均变化率最大的是\dotfill{(\kh{B})}
        \xx{$y = {x}^{2}$}{$y = {x}^{3}$}{$y = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{x}$}{$y = {2}^{x}$}
        
        \begin{jieda}
            对于 ${A},y = {x}^{2}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-1,0}\right\rbrack$ 上的平均变化率为 $\dfrac{0 - 1}{0 - \left( {-1}\right) } =  - 1$ ,
            
            对于 ${B},y = {x}^{3}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-1,0}\right\rbrack$ 上的平均变化率为 $\dfrac{0 - \left( {-1}\right) }{0 - \left( {-1}\right) } = 1$ ,
            
            对于 ${C},y = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{x}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-1,0}\right\rbrack$ 上的平均变化率为 $\dfrac{1 - {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{-1}}{0 - \left( {-1}\right) } =  - 1$ ,
            
            对于 ${D},y = {2}^{x}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-1,0}\right\rbrack$ 上的平均变化率为 $\dfrac{1 - {2}^{-1}}{0 - \left( {-1}\right) } = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,
            
            故 $y = {x}^{3}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-1,0}\right\rbrack$ 上的平均变化率最大
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知某容器的高度为 20cm，现在向容器内注入液体，且容器内液体的高度 $h$ (单位: ${{cm}}$ ) 与时间 $t$ (单位: ${s}$ ) 的函数关系式为 $h = \dfrac{1}{3}{t}^{3} + {t}^{2}$ ,当 $t = {t}_{0}$ 时,液体上升高度的瞬时变化率为 $3{{cm}}/{s}$ ,则当 $t = {t}_{0} + 1$ 时，液体上升高度的瞬时变化率为\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        \xx{$5{{cm}}/{s}$}{$6{{cm}}/{s}$}{$8{{cm}}/{s}$}{${10}{{cm}}/{s}$}

        \begin{jieda}
            由 $h = \dfrac{1}{3}{t}^{3} + {t}^{2}$ ,求导得: ${h}^{\prime } = {t}^{2} + {2t}$ .
            
            当 $t = {t}_{0}$ 时， ${h}^{\prime } = {t}_{0}^{2} + 2{t}_{0} = 3$ ，解得 ${t}_{0} = 1\left( {{t}_{0} =  - 3}\right.$ 舍去 $)$ .
            
            故当 $t = {t}_{0} + 1 = 2$ 时,液体上升高度的瞬时变化率为 ${2}^{2} + 2 \times  2 = 8{{cm}}/{s}$ .
        \end{jieda}
        \item 一个球的半径以 2 厘米/秒的速度在增长，当此球的半径为 6 厘米时，其体积的变化率为\dotfill{(\kh{B})}
        \xx{$576\pi {cm}^{3}/$ 秒}{$288\pi {cm}^{3}/$ 秒}{$144\pi {cm}^{3}/$ 秒}{${96\pi } {cm}^{3}/$ 秒}
        
        \item 一个圆的半径以 0.1 厘米/秒的速度在增长，若圆的周长为 $C$ 厘米，则其面积的变化率为\dotfill{(\kh{B})}
        \xx{$0.2\pi C {cm}^{2}/$ 秒}{$0.1C {cm}^{2}/$ 秒}{$\dfrac{0.1C}{2\pi} {cm}^{2}/$ 秒}{$-\dfrac{0.1C}{2\pi} {cm}^{2}/$ 秒}
    \end{enumerate}
    
    \item 利用导数的定义解题
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 若 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  = 4$ ，则 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {2 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( {2 - {\Delta x}}\right) }{\Delta x} =$ \blkda{8}
        \begin{jieda}
            由题意知, ${f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  = 4$，则 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {2 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( {2 - {\Delta x}}\right) }{\Delta x} = 2\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {2 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( {2 - {\Delta x}}\right) }{2\Delta x} = 2{f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  = 8$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 设 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $R$ 上的导函数为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,若 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {3 - {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 3\right) }{3\Delta x} = 2$，则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)  =$\blkda{$-6$}
        \begin{jieda}
            由于 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {3 - {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 3\right) }{3\Delta x} =  - \dfrac{1}{3}\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {3 - {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 3\right) }{-{\Delta x}} =  - \dfrac{1}{3}{f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)  = 2$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)  =  - 6$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 若 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x + {x}^{3}$ ，则 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {1 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{2\Delta x} =$\blkda{2}
        \begin{jieda}
            根据导数的定义可得 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {1 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{2\Delta x} = \dfrac{1}{2}\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {1 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{\Delta x} = \dfrac{1}{2}{f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)$ ,
            
            由 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x + {x}^{3}$ 可得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} + 3{x}^{2}$ ,
            
            可得 $\dfrac{1}{2}{f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {\dfrac{1}{1} + 3 \times  {1}^{2}}\right)  = 2$ ,即 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {1 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{2\Delta x} = 2$ .
        \end{jieda}

    \end{enumerate}

    \item 求曲线切线的斜率 (倾斜角)
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 设 $f\left( x\right)$ 是可导函数,且 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {1 + {3\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{\Delta x} = 2$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 1$ 处的切线的斜率等于\blkda{$\dfrac{2}{3}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {1 + {3\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{\Delta x} = 2$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {1 + {3\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{3\Delta x} = \dfrac{1}{3}\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {1 + {3\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{\Delta x} = \dfrac{2}{3}$ , 由导数的几何意义知, $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 1$ 处的切线的斜率为 $\dfrac{2}{3}$
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 曲线 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{9}{x}$ 在点 $\left( {3,3}\right)$ 处的切线的倾斜角 $\alpha$ 等于\blkda{${135}^{ \circ  }$}
        \begin{jieda}
            ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {x + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( x\right) }{\Delta x} = 9\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{\dfrac{1}{x + {\Delta x}}}{\Delta x} =  - 9\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{1}{\left( {x + {\Delta x}}\right) {\Delta x}} =  - \dfrac{9}{{x}^{2}}$ ,
            
            所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)  =  - 1$ . 又切线的倾斜角 $\alpha$ 的范围为 ${0}^{ \circ  } \leq  \alpha  \leq  {180}^{ \circ  }$ ,所以所求倾斜角为 ${135}^{ \circ  }$ .
        \end{jieda}

        \item 函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象如图所示，下列关系正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.25\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5nlf73ef24c73bmm510_4_143_1619_379_287_0.jpg}
        \end{center}

        \xx{$0 < {f}^{\prime }\left( 4\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 5\right)  < f\left( 5\right)  - f\left( 4\right)$}{$0 < {f}^{\prime }\left( 4\right)  < f\left( 5\right)  - f\left( 4\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 5\right)$}{$0 < {f}^{\prime }\left( 5\right)  < f\left( 5\right)  - f\left( 4\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 4\right)$}{$0 < f\left( 5\right)  - f\left( 4\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 5\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 4\right)$}
        \begin{jieda}
            根据题意 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 4\right)$ 的几何意义为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $B$ 处切线的斜率,
            
            ${f}^{\prime }\left( 5\right)$ 的几何意义为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $A$ 处切线的斜率,
            
            $f\left( 5\right)  - f\left( 4\right)  = \dfrac{f\left( 5\right)  - f\left( 4\right) }{5 - 4}$ ，其几何意义为割线 ${AB}$ 的斜率，
            
            则有 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 5\right)  < f\left( 5\right)  - f\left( 4\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 4\right)$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 设 $f\left( x\right)$ 为可导函数，且满足 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {3 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 3\right) }{3\Delta x} = 2$ ，则曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {3,f\left( 3\right) }\right)$ 处的切线的斜率是\blkda{6}
        \begin{jieda}
            由题意, $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {3 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 3\right) }{3\Delta x} = \dfrac{1}{3}\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( {3 + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( 3\right) }{\Delta x} = 2$ ,
            
            即 $\dfrac{1}{3}{f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)  = 2$ ,故 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)  = 6$ ,即曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {3,f\left( 3\right) }\right)$ 处的切线的斜率是 6 .
        \end{jieda}

    \end{enumerate}
    \item 函数图象与导函数的关系
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象如图所示,下列数值排序正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
        
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5nlf73ef24c73bmm510_10_142_1485_349_326_0.jpg}
        \end{center}

        \xx{${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)  > 0$}{${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)  < 0$}{$0 < {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)$}{$0 > {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)$}
        
        \begin{jieda}
            作曲线在点 $\left( {1,f\left( 1\right) }\right) ,\left( {2,f\left( 2\right) }\right) ,\left( {3,f\left( 3\right) }\right)$ 处的切线,记其斜率依次为 ${k}_{1},{k}_{2},{k}_{3}$ , 结合图象可得 $0 > {k}_{1} > {k}_{2} > {k}_{3}$ 
                        
            \begin{center}
            \includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5nlf73ef24c73bmm510_11_143_674_344_317_0.jpg}
            \end{center}
            
            由导数的几何意义可得 ${k}_{1} = {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right) ,{k}_{2} = {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right) ,{k}_{3} = {f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)$
            
            所以 $0 > {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)$ .
        \end{jieda}
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的部分图象如图所示, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 为 $f\left( x\right)$ 的导函数, 则\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
        
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5nlf73ef24c73bmm510_8_140_1394_322_292_0.jpg}
        \end{center}

        \xx{$f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( 0\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)$}{${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  > f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( 0\right)$}{${f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  > f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( 0\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)$}{${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  > f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( 0\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)$}

        \begin{jieda}
            由导数的几何意义可知, ${f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)$ 表示曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 0$ 处的切线斜率，${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)$ 表示曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 1$ 处的切线斜率，
            
            $\dfrac{f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( 0\right) }{1 - 0}$ 表示 $\left( {0,f\left( 0\right) }\right) ,\left( {1,f\left( 1\right) }\right)$ 两点连线的斜率,
            
            由图可知，当 $x$ 从 0 变化到 1 时， $f\left( x\right)$ 切线斜率越来越大，
            
            所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  > f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( 0\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)$ ,对比选项可知, ${D}$ 正确.
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象如图所示，下列数值排序正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{B})}
        
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5nlf73ef24c73bmm510_9_138_577_303_330_0.jpg}
        \end{center}

        \xx{$0 < {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  < f\left( 2\right)  - f\left( 1\right)$}{$0 < {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  < f\left( 2\right)  - f\left( 1\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)$}{$0 < {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  < f\left( 2\right)  - f\left( 1\right)$}{$0 < f\left( 2\right)  - f\left( 1\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)$}
        \begin{jieda}
            如图,设函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象上有两点 $A\left( {1,f\left( 1\right) }\right) ,B\left( {2,f\left( 2\right) }\right)$ ,经过点 $A,B$ 的切线分别为 ${l}_{A},{l}_{B}$
            \begin{center}
            \includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5nlf73ef24c73bmm510_9_140_1196_349_329_0.jpg}
            \end{center}
            则直线 ${AB},{l}_{A},{l}_{B}$ 的斜率依次为 ${k}_{AB} = \dfrac{f\left( 2\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{2 - 1} = f\left( 2\right)  - f\left( 1\right) ,{k}_{{l}_{A}} = {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right) ,{k}_{{l}_{B}} = {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)$ ,
            
            由图知直线 ${AB},{l}_{A},{l}_{B}$ 的倾斜角 ${\alpha }_{AB},{\alpha }_{{l}_{A}},{\alpha }_{{l}_{B}}$ 满足, $0 < {\alpha }_{{l}_{B}} < {\alpha }_{AB} < {\alpha }_{{l}_{A}} < \dfrac{{\pi }}{2}$ ,
            
            因函数 $y = \tan x$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 上递增，故 $0 < \tan {\alpha }_{{l}_{B}} < \tan {\alpha }_{AB} < \tan {\alpha }_{{l}_{A}}$ ,
            
            即 $0 < {f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  < f\left( 2\right)  - f\left( 1\right)  < {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        
        \item 如图是函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的部分图象，记 $f\left( x\right)$ 的导数为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ，则下列选项中值最大的是\dotfill{(\kh{A})}
        
        \begin{center}
        \includegraphics[width=0.3\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5nlf73ef24c73bmm510_10_143_271_358_241_0.jpg}
        \end{center}

        \xx{$f\left( 3\right)$}{$3{f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)$}{$f\left( {-{14}}\right)$}{${f}^{\prime }\left( 8\right)$}
        \begin{jieda}
            由图可知, $f\left( {-{14}}\right) ,{f}^{\prime }\left( 8\right)$ 为负数, $f\left( 3\right) ,3{f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)$ 为正数,故不选 $f\left( {-{14}}\right) ,{f}^{\prime }\left( 8\right)$ 
            
            \begin{center}
            \includegraphics[width=0.3\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5nlf73ef24c73bmm510_10_143_756_358_244_0.jpg}
            \end{center}
            
            设 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 3$ 处的点为 $A$ ，显然 ${OA}$ 的斜率 ${k}_{OA}$ 大于 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)$ ，
            
            则 $\dfrac{f\left( 3\right)  - 0}{3 - 0} > {f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)$ ，可转化为 $f\left( 3\right)  > 3{f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)$ ，
            
            所以 $f\left( 3\right)$ 的值最大.
        \end{jieda}
    \end{enumerate}
    
    \item 基本初等函数的导数

    \begin{enumerate}
    \item 下列求导运算结果错误的是\dotfill{(\kh{A})}
    \xx{${\left( \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}$}{${\left( \ln x\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{x}$}{${\left( {{e}}^{x}\right) }^{\prime } = {{e}}^{x}$}{${\left( \sin x\right) }^{\prime } = \cos x$}

    \begin{jieda}
        对于 ${A},{\left( \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{\prime } =  - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}$ ,故 ${A}$ 错误;
        
        对于 ${B},{\left( \ln x\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,故 ${B}$ 正确;
        
        对于 ${C},{\left( {{e}}^{x}\right) }^{\prime } = {{e}}^{x}$ ,故 ${C}$ 正确;
        
        对于 ${D},{\left( \sin x\right) }^{\prime } = \cos x$ ,故 ${D}$ 正确.
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 下列导数运算正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
    \xx{${\left( \cos 3\right) }^{\prime } =  - \sin 3$}{${\left( {e}^{3}\right) }^{\prime } = 3{e}^{3}$}{${\left( \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{\prime } =  - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}$}{${\left( {\log }_{2}x\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{x}$}

    \begin{jieda}
        因为 ${\left( \cos 3\right) }^{\prime } = 0,{\left( {{e}}^{3}\right) }^{\prime } = 0,{\left( \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{\prime } =  - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}},{\left( {\log }_{2}x\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{x\ln 2}$ .
    \end{jieda}
    
    \item 下列求导运算正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
    \xx{${\left( \ln 2\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{2}$}{${\left( {e}^{3}\right) }^{\prime } = {3e}$}{${\left( {a}^{x}\right) }^{\prime } = x \cdot  {a}^{x - 1}$}{${\left( \cos x\right) }^{\prime } =  - \sin x$}
    \begin{jieda}
        对于 ${A} : {\left( \ln 2\right) }^{\prime } = 0$ ,故 ${A}$ 错误;
        
        对于 ${B} : {\left( {{e}}^{3}\right) }^{\prime } = 0$ ,故 ${B}$ 错误;
        
        对于 ${C} : {\left( {a}^{x}\right) }^{\prime } = {a}^{x} \cdot  \ln a$ ,故 ${C}$ 错误;
        
        对于 ${D} : {\left( \cos x\right) }^{\prime } =  - \sin x$ ，故 ${D}$ 正确
    \end{jieda}
    
    \end{enumerate}

    \item 导数的四则运算法则
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 下列导数运算正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{B})}
        \xx{${\left( \sin x\right) }^{\prime } =  - \cos x$}{${\left( \dfrac{{{e}}^{x}}{x}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{x{{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{x}}{{x}^{2}}$}{${\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{\prime } = {2}^{x}$}{${\left( \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}}\right) }^{\prime } =  - \dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{x}}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            对于 ${A},{\left( \sin x\right) }^{\prime } = \cos x$ ,故 ${A}$ 错误;
            
            对于 ${B},{\left( \dfrac{{{e}}^{x}}{x}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{x{{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{x}}{{x}^{2}}$ ，故 ${B}$ 正确；
            
            对于 ${C},{\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{\prime } = {2}^{x} \cdot  \ln 2$ ,故 ${C}$ 错误;
            
            对于 ${D},{\left( \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}}\right) }^{\prime } = {\left( {x}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right) }^{\prime } =  - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}^{-\frac{3}{2}}$ ,故 ${D}$ 错误.

        \end{jieda}

        \item 下列求导运算正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
        \xx{${\left( \cos x\right) }^{\prime } = \sin x$}{${\left( {x}^{2} + \ln 3\right) }^{\prime } = {2x} + \dfrac{1}{3}$}{${\left( {x}^{3}{{e}}^{x}\right) }^{\prime } = \sin x$}{${\left( \sqrt{x}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{x}}$}

        \begin{jieda}
            对于 ${A},{\left( \cos x\right) }^{\prime } =  - \sin x$ ,所以 ${A}$ 错误,
            
            对于 ${B},\left( {{x}^{2} + \ln 3}\right)  = {2x}$ ,所以 ${B}$ 错误,
            
            对于 ${C},{\left( {x}^{3}{{e}}^{x}\right) }^{\prime } = 3{x}^{2}{{e}}^{x} + {x}^{3}{{e}}^{x}$ ,所以 ${C}$ 错误,
            
            对于 ${D},{\left( \sqrt{x}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{2}{x}^{-\frac{1}{2}} = \dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{x}}$ ，所以 ${D}$ 正确.
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - f'\left( 1\right) x$ ，则\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        \xx{$f\left( 1\right)  =  - \dfrac{{e}}{2}$}{$f'\left( 1\right)  =  - \dfrac{{e}}{2}$}{$f\left( 2\right)  = {{e}}^{2} - {e}$}{$f'\left( 2\right)  = {{e}}^{2} - {e}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - f'\left( 1\right) x$ ,所以 $f'\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - f'\left( 1\right)$，则 $f'\left( 1\right)  = {e} - f'\left( 1\right)$ ，所以 $f'\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{{e}}{2}$
            
            则 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - \dfrac{{e}}{2}x$ ,所以 $f\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{{e}}{2},f\left( 2\right)  = {{e}}^{2} - \dfrac{{e}}{2},f'\left( 2\right)  = {{e}}^{2} - {e}$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} + f'\left( 1\right) {x}^{2} + f\left( 1\right) x + 1$ ,则 $f'\left( 2\right)  =$\blkda{$e-7$}
        \begin{jieda}
            令 $x = 1$ ,得 $f\left( 1\right)  = 1 + f'\left( 1\right)  + f\left( 1\right)  + 1$ ,解得 $f'\left( 1\right)  =  - 2$ ,
            
            所以 $f'\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - {4x} + f\left( 1\right)$ ,
            
            令 $x = 1$ ,得 $f'\left( 1\right)  = 1 - 4 + f\left( 1\right)  =  - 2$ ,解得 $f\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,
            
            所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - 2{x}^{2} + x + 1$ ,所以 $f'\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - {4x} + 1$ ,所以 $f'\left( 2\right)  = {e} - 7$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = x\left( {x - 1}\right) \left( {x - 2}\right) \left( {x - 3}\right) \left( {x - 4}\right) \left( {x - 5}\right)$ ,求 $f'\left( 2\right)  =$\blkda{$-12$}
        \begin{jieda}
            令 $g\left( x\right)  = x\left( {x - 1}\right) \left( {x - 3}\right) \left( {x - 4}\right) \left( {x - 5}\right)$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)  = \left( {x - 2}\right) g\left( x\right)$ ,两边求导得到$f'\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)  + \left( {x - 2}\right) {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,令 $x = 2$ ,得到 $f'\left( 2\right)  = g\left( 2\right)  =  - {12}$ .
        \end{jieda}
    \end{enumerate}
    \item 复合函数的求导方法
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 下列求导运算不正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{A})}
        \xx{${\left( {x}^{2}\sin x + 1\right) }^{\prime } = {2x}\cos x$}{${\left( \sqrt{{2x} - 1}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{{2x} - 1}}$}{${\left\lbrack  \ln \left( 1 - 2x\right) \right\rbrack  }^{\prime } = \dfrac{2}{{2x} - 1}$}{${\left( \dfrac{x}{{{e}}^{x}}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1 - x}{{{e}}^{x}}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            对于 ${A} : {\left( {x}^{2}\sin x + 1\right) }^{\prime } = {2x}\sin x + {x}^{2}\cos x$ , ${A}$ 错误;
            
            对于 ${B}$ ,令 ${\left( \sqrt{{2x} - 1}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{2}{\left( 2x - 1\right) }^{-\frac{1}{2}} \cdot  2 = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{{2x} - 1}}$ , B 正确;
            
            对于 ${C} : {\left\lbrack  \ln \left( 1 - 2x\right) \right\rbrack  }^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{1 - {2x}} \cdot  \left( {-2}\right)  = \dfrac{2}{{2x} - 1},{C}$ 正确;
            
            对于 ${D} : {\left( \dfrac{x}{{{e}}^{x}}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{{{e}}^{x} - x{{e}}^{x}}{{\left( {{e}}^{x}\right) }^{2}} = \dfrac{1 - x}{{{e}}^{x}}$ , ${D}$ 正确.
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 下列求导不正确的是\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        \xx{${\left\lbrack  {\left( 3x + 5\right) }^{3}\right\rbrack  }^{\prime } = 9{\left( 3x + 5\right) }^{2}$}{${\left( {{e}}^{2}\right) }^{\prime } = 0$}{${\left( \dfrac{2\sin x}{{x}^{2}}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{{2x}\cos x + 4\sin x}{{x}^{3}}$}{${\left( {2}^{x} + \cos x\right) }^{\prime } = {2}^{x}\ln 2 - \sin x$}
        \begin{jieda}
            对于 ${A},{\left( {\left( 3x + 5\right) }^{3}\right) }^{\prime } = {\left( 3x + 5\right) }^{\prime } \cdot  3{\left( 3x + 5\right) }^{2} = 9{\left( 3x + 5\right) }^{2}$ ,故 ${A}$ 正确；
            
            对于 ${B},{{e}}^{2}$ 是常数,导数为 0,故 ${B}$ 正确;
            
            对于 ${C},{\left( \dfrac{2\sin x}{{x}^{2}}\right) }^{\prime } = \dfrac{{\left( 2\sin x\right) }^{\prime }{x}^{2} - 2\sin x \cdot  {\left( {x}^{2}\right) }^{\prime }}{{x}^{4}} = \dfrac{{2x}\cos x - 4\sin x}{{x}^{3}}$ ,故 ${C}$ 错误;
            
            对于 ${D},{\left( {2}^{x} + \cos x\right) }^{\prime } = {\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{\prime } + {\left( \cos x\right) }^{\prime } = {2}^{x}\ln 2 - \sin x$ ,故 ${D}$ 正确.
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 设 ${f}_{0}\left( x\right)  = \sin {2x} + \cos {2x},{f}_{1}\left( x\right)  = {f}_{0}^{\prime }\left( x\right) ,{f}_{2}\left( x\right)  = {f}_{1}^{\prime }\left( x\right) ,\cdots  {f}_{n + 1}\left( x\right)  = {f}_{n}^{\prime }\left( x\right) ,n \in  {N},$ 则 ${f}_{2020}\left( x\right)  =$\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        \xx{${2}^{2020}\left( {\cos {2x} - \sin {2x}}\right)$}{ ${2}^{2021}\left( {-\sin {2x} - \cos {2x}}\right)$}{${2}^{2020}\left( {\sin {2x} + \cos {2x}}\right)$}{${2}^{2021}\left( {-\cos {2x} + \sin {2x}}\right)$}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 ${f}_{0}\left( x\right)  = \sin {2x} + \cos {2x}$ ,
            
            所以 ${f}_{1}\left( x\right)  = {f}_{0}\left( x\right)  = 2\cos {2x} - 2\sin {2x} = 2\left( {\cos {2x} - \sin {2x}}\right)$ ,
            
            ${f}_{2}\left( x\right)  = {f}_{1}\left( x\right)  = {2}^{2}\left( {-\sin {2x} - \cos {2x}}\right)$ ,
            
            ${f}_{3}\left( x\right)  = {f}_{2}\left( x\right)  = {2}^{3}\left( {-\cos {2x} + \sin {2x}}\right)$ ,
            
            ${f}_{4}\left( x\right)  = {f}_{3}\left( x\right)  = {2}^{4}\left( {\sin {2x} + \cos {2x}}\right) ,$
            
            ...
            
            因为 ${2020} = {505} \times  4$ ,
            
            所以 ${f}_{2020}\left( x\right)  = {2}^{2020}\left( {\sin {2x} + \cos {2x}}\right)$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 设定义在 $R$ 上的函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的导函数为 $f'\left( x\right)$ ，且 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  = {2f}\left( x\right) ,f\left( x\right)  > 0$ ，则 $\dfrac{f'\left( 5\right) }{f'\left( 0\right) } =$ \blkda{$32$}

        \begin{jieda}
            $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  = {2f}\left( x\right)$ 两边对 $x$ 求导,得 $f'\left( {x + 1}\right)  \cdot  {\left( x + 1\right) }^{\prime } = {2f}'\left( x\right)$ ,即 $\dfrac{f'\left( {x + 1}\right) }{f'\left( x\right) } = 2$ 
            
            所以 $\dfrac{f'\left( 1\right) }{f'\left( 0\right) } = 2,\dfrac{f'\left( 2\right) }{f'\left( 1\right) } = 2,\cdots ,\dfrac{f'\left( 5\right) }{f'\left( 4\right) } = 2$ ,累乘可得 $\dfrac{f'\left( 5\right) }{f'\left( 0\right) } = {2}^{5} = {32}$ .
        \end{jieda}
    \end{enumerate}

    \item 函数图象的判断及应用
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)  = x\sin x + \cos x$ 的导数 $f'\left( x\right)$ 的部分图象大致为\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
        \xx{\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_7_247_1844_285_275_0.jpg}}
        {\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_7_763_1839_295_279_0.jpg}}
        {\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_8_243_210_286_272_0.jpg}}
        {\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_8_767_206_292_276_0.jpg}}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $f\left( x\right)  = x\sin x + \cos x$ ,所以 $f'\left( x\right)  = \sin x + x\cos x - \sin x = x\cos x$ ,
            
            令 $g\left( x\right)  = f'\left( x\right)  = x\cos x,x \in  {R}$ ，则 $g\left( {-x}\right)  =  - x\cos x =  - g\left( x\right)$ ，
            
            所以函数 $g\left( x\right)  = x\cos x$ 是奇函数，故 A，C 错误；
            
            又 $g\left( {\pi }\right)  = {\pi }\cos {\pi } =  - {\pi } < 0$ ,故 ${B}$ 错误.
        \end{jieda}

        \item 若函数 $f\left( x\right)  = a{x}^{2} + {bx} + c\left( {a > 0}\right)$ 的图象的顶点在第三象限，则函数 $f'\left( x\right)$ 的图象大致是\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        \xx{\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_8_243_1106_270_256_0.jpg}}
        {\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_8_764_1103_276_259_0.jpg}}
        {\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_8_241_1373_270_256_0.jpg}}
        {\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_8_766_1369_274_258_0.jpg}}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为函数 $f\left( x\right)  = a{x}^{2} + {bx} + c\left( {a > 0}\right)$ 的图象的顶点在第三象限，则 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} a > 0 \\   - \dfrac{b}{2a} < 0 \\  \dfrac{{4ac} - {b}^{2}}{4a} < 0 \end{matrix}\right.$ ，所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} a > 0 \\  b > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ，又 $f\left( x\right)  = {2ax} + b$ ，则 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象单调递增，且与 $y$ 轴交于正半轴.
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 设函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  - \cos x - {x}^{4}$ 的导函数为 $g\left( x\right)$ ,则 $\left| {g\left( x\right) }\right|$ 图象大致是\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
        \xx{\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_9_254_284_215_178_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_9_778_278_208_183_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_9_253_506_233_175_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.35\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_9_770_500_261_185_0.jpg}}
        
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $f\left( x\right)  =  - \cos x - {x}^{4}$ ,所以 $f'\left( x\right)  = \sin x - 4{x}^{3}$ ,所以 $g\left( x\right)  = \sin x - 4{x}^{3}$ ,
            
            所以函数 $g\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数，其图象关于原点成中心对称，而函数 $\left| {g\left( x\right) }\right|$ 为偶函数，其图象关于 $y$ 轴对称，所以选项 $B,C$ 错误; 又因为其图象过原点 $O$ ,所以舍去 $A$
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 下列四个图象中，有一个图象是函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}{x}^{3} - a{x}^{2} + \left( {{a}^{2} - 4}\right) x + 8\left( {a \neq  0}\right)$ 的导数的图象，则 $f\left( {-2}\right)$ 的值为\blkda{$-\dfrac{8}{3}$}
        \fourtu{\includegraphics[width=0.4\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_9_142_1210_222_258_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.4\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_9_392_1210_222_258_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.4\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_9_649_1210_222_258_0.jpg}}{\includegraphics[width=0.4\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/bo_d5o6oibef24c73bmtjs0_9_922_1210_222_258_0.jpg}}
        \begin{jieda}
            函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}{x}^{3} - a{x}^{2} + \left( {{a}^{2} - 4}\right) x + 8$ ,求导得 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - {2ax} + {a}^{2} - 4 = {\left( x - a\right) }^{2} - 4$，于是函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象是开口向上,对称轴为 $x = a$ 的抛物线, \ding{172}\ding{173} 不满足,
            
            又 $a \neq  0$ ，即函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象对称轴不是 $y$ 轴， \ding{175} 不满足，因此符合条件的是 \ding{174}，
            
            函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图象过原点,且 $a > 0$ ,显然 $f'\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,从而 $a = 2$，$f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}{x}^{3} - 2{x}^{2} + 8$ ，所以 $f\left( {-2}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3} \times  {\left( -2\right) }^{3} - 2 \times  {\left( -2\right) }^{2} + 8 =  - \dfrac{8}{3}$ .
        \end{jieda}
    \end{enumerate}
    
    \item 求在曲线上一点的切线方程、过一点的曲线方程
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 曲线 $y = \ln x$ 在点 $\left( {{e},1}\right)$ 处的切线方程为\blkda{$y = \dfrac{1}{{e}}x$}
        \begin{jieda}
            已知 $y = \ln x$ ,函数定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$，可得 ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{x}$，此时 ${y}^{\prime }{|}_{x = {e}} = \dfrac{1}{{e}}$，所以曲线 $y = \ln x$ 在点 $\left( {{e},7}\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y - 1 = \dfrac{1}{{e}}\left( {x - {e}}\right)$，即 $y = \dfrac{1}{{e}}x$
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知曲线$y = \dfrac{x^3}{3}$，求过点$P\left(2, \dfrac{8}{3}\right)$的切线方程.
        \begin{jieda}
            设切点的坐标为 $\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,切点在曲线上，在点 $\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 处切线的斜率为 $k = {y}^{\prime } = {x}_{0}^{2}$，切线方程为 $y - {y}_{0} = {x}_{0}^{2}\left( {x - {x}_{0}}\right)$ .
            
            又因为切线过点 $P\left( {2,\dfrac{8}{3}}\right)$ ,且切点 $\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 在曲线 $y = \dfrac{1}{3}{x}^{3}$ 上，故$\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{8}{3} - {y}_{0} = {x}_{0}^{2}\left( {2 - {x}_{0}}\right) , \\  {y}_{0} = \dfrac{1}{3}{x}_{0}^{3}, \end{array}\right.$ 解得 ${x}_{0} = 2$ 或 ${x}_{0} =  - 1$ .
            \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                \item 当 ${x}_{0} = 2,{y}_{0} = \dfrac{8}{3}$ ,即切线斜率为 4 时,切线的方程为 ${12x} - {3y} - {16} = 0$
                \item 当 ${x}_{0} =  - 1,{y}_{0} =  - \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,即切线斜率为 1 时,切线的方程为 ${3x} - {3y} + 2 = 0$
            \end{dingautolist}
            综上,所求切线方程为 ${12x} - {3y} - {16} = 0$ 或 ${3x} - {3y} + 2 = 0$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + \ln x$ 在点 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线方程是\blkda[3]{${3x} - y - 2 = 0$}
        \begin{jieda}
            由 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + \ln x$ ,得到 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} + \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = 2 + 1 = 3$，所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + \ln x$ 在点 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线方程是 $y - 1 = 3\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,即 ${3x} - y - 2 = 0$
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 过点$(1,4)$且与曲线 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} + x + 2$ 相切的直线方程为\blkda[5]{${4x} - y = 0$ 或 ${7x} - {4y} + 9 = 0$}
        \begin{jieda}
            设过点 $\left( {1,4}\right)$ 的曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的切线为: $l : y - {y}_{0} = \left( {3{x}_{0}^{2} + 1}\right) \left( {x - {x}_{0}}\right)$ ,有 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} \left( {3{x}_{0}^{2} + 1}\right) \left( {1 - {x}_{0}}\right)  = 4 - {y}_{0} \\  {y}_{0} = {x}_{0}^{3} + {x}_{0} + 2 \end{matrix}\right.$，解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{0} = 1 \\  {y}_{0} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{0} =  - \dfrac{1}{2} \\  {y}_{0} = \dfrac{9}{8} \end{array}\right.$，代入 $l$ 可得 ${4x} - y = 0$ 或 ${7x} - {4y} + 9 = 0$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2}{f}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right)  + \ln x - 9$ ，则函数在 $x = 1$ 处的切线方程是\blkda[3]{$y = {19x} - {19}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 2{f}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) x + \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,令 $x = \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,可得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right)  = 9$ ,
            
            $\therefore f\left( 1\right)  = 9 + 0 - 9 = 0,{f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = {19}$ ,
            
            所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 1$ 处的切线方程为 $y = {19x} - {19}$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 曲线 $y = \dfrac{x}{{2x} - 1}$ 在点 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线方程为\blkda[3]{$x + y - 2 = 0$}
        \begin{jieda}
            求导可得 ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{{2x} - 1 - {2x}}{{\left( 2x - 1\right) }^{2}} = \dfrac{-1}{{\left( 2x - 1\right) }^{2}}$，所以切线的斜率为 $k =  - 1$ ,
            
            所以切线方程为 $y - 1 =  - \left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,即 $x + y - 2 = 0$
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 曲线 $f\left( x\right)  = \left( {x - 2}\right) {{e}}^{x} - {x}^{2} + {2x}$ 在 $x = 2$ 处的切线方程是\blkda[3]{$y = \left( {{{e}}^{2} - 2}\right) \left( {x - 2}\right)$}
        \begin{jieda}
            由函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left( {x - 2}\right) {{e}}^{x} - {x}^{2} + {2x}$ ,可得 $f\left( x\right)  = \left( {x - 1}\right) {{e}}^{x} - {2x} + 2$ ,
            
            则 $f\left( 2\right)  = 0$ 且 $f\left( 2\right)  = {{e}}^{2} - 2$ ，即切点为 $\left( {2,0}\right)$ ，切线的斜率为 ${{e}}^{2} - 2$ ，
            
            所以函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 2$ 处的切线方程为 $y = \left( {{{e}}^{2} - 2}\right) \left( {x - 2}\right)$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 若函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x - {ax} + 1$ 的图象在 $x = 2$ 处的切线与 $y$ 轴垂直，则函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象在 $x = 1$ 处的切线方程为\blkda[3]{$x - {2y} = 0$}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x - {ax} + 1,x > 0$ ,

            所以 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - a$ ,
            
            又因为函数在 $x = 2$ 处的切线与 $y$ 轴垂直，
            
            所以 $f\left( 2\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2} - a = 0$ ,
            
            解得 $a = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,
            
            所以 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x - \dfrac{1}{2}x + 1,x > 0$ ,
            
            所以 $f\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,
            
            即切点为 $\left( {1,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,
            
            又因为 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ，
            
            所以在 $x = 1$ 处的切线的斜率 $k = f\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,
            
            所以在 $x = 1$ 处的切线方程为: $y - \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {x - 1}\right)$，即 $y = \dfrac{1}{2}x,x - {2y} = 0$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln \left( {-{2x}}\right)$ ，则 $f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {{x}_{0},f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }\right)$ 处切线的斜率为\blkda{$\dfrac{1}{{x}_{0}}$}
    \end{enumerate}
    
    \item 已知切线 (斜率) 求参数
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 已知函数$f(x) = \dfrac{1}{2}x - \dfrac{1}{4}sinx-\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{4}cosx$的图象在$A(x_0, y_0)$的切线斜率为$1$，则$tanx_0 = $\blkda{$-\sqrt{3}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            函数的导数$f'(x) = \dfrac{1}{2}-\dfrac{1}{4}cosx+\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{4}sinx$，令$f'(x_0) = 1$，即$-\dfrac{1}{2}cosx_0 + \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}sinx_0 = 1$，根据辅助角公式有$sin\left(x_0 - \dfrac{\pi}{6}\right) = 1$，因此$x_0 = 2k\pi + \dfrac{2\pi}{3}, k \in \mathbf{Z}$，所以$tanx_0 = -\sqrt{3}$
        \end{jieda}
        \item 已知函数$f(x) = lnx - \dfrac{a}{x} - 1$，若曲线$y = f(x)$存在斜率为$-1$的切线，求实数$a$的取值范围.
        \begin{jieda}
            $f'(x) = \dfrac{1}{x} + \dfrac{a}{x^2} (x > 0)$，令$f'(x) = -1$，即$x^2+x+a = 0$存在大于$0$的实数根. 易知$y = x^2+x+a$在$(0, +\infty)$单调递增，因此$a \in (-\infty, 0)$
        \end{jieda}
        \item 设函数$f(x) = ax+\dfrac{1}{x+b}(a, b \in \mathbf{Z})$，曲线$y = f(x)$在点$(2, f(2))$处的切线方程为$y = 3$
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 求$y = f(x)$的表达式.
            \item 证明：曲线$y = f(x)$上任意一点的切线与直线$x = 1$和直线$y = x$所围成的三角形面积为定值，求此定值.
        \end{enumerate}
        \begin{jieda}
            \begin{enumerate}
                \item $\left \{ \begin{array}{l}
                     f(2) = 2a+\dfrac{1}{2+b} = 3 \\
                     f'(2) = a-\dfrac{1}{(2+b)^2} = 0
                \end{array} \right.$，解得$\left \{ \begin{array}{l}
                     a = 1 \\
                     b = -1
                \end{array} \right.$或$\left \{ \begin{array}{l}
                     a = \dfrac{9}{4} \\
                     b = -\dfrac{8}{3}
                \end{array} \right.$，又因为$a, b \in \mathbf{Z}$，所以$f(x) = x + \dfrac{1}{x-1}$
                \item $f'(x) = 1 - \dfrac{1}{(x-1)^2}$，故$\left(x_0, x_0+\dfrac{1}{x_0-1}\right)$处的切线为$y = \left(1 - \dfrac{1}{(x_0-1)^2}\right)(x-x_0)+\left(x_0+\dfrac{1}{x_0-1}\right)$ \\
                通过联立方程可知切线与$x = 1$与$y = x$的交点为$A\left(1, \dfrac{x_0+1}{x_0-1}\right)$和$B(2x_0-1, 2x_0-1)$，直线$x=1$与$y = x$的交点为$C(1,1)$，故$\vv{CA} = \left(0, \dfrac{2}{x_0-1}\right)$，$\vv{CB} = (2x_0-2, 2x_0 - 2)$，故三角形面积$S = \dfrac{1}{2}\left|(2x_0-2)\cdot\left(\dfrac{2}{x_0-1}\right) - 0 \cdot (2x_0-2)\right| = 2$
            \end{enumerate}
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 若曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 1$ 处的切线的斜率为 -3,则 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( {1 + {2\Delta x}}\right) }{\Delta x} =$\blkda{6}
        \begin{jieda}
             $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( {1 + {2\Delta x}}\right) }{\Delta x} =  - 2\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0 }}\dfrac{f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( {1 + {2\Delta x}}\right) }{-{2\Delta x}} =  - 2{f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = 6$
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left( {x - a}\right) \left( {x - 2}\right) \left( {x - 3}\right) \left( {x - 4}\right)$ ，若 $f\left( x\right)$ 的图象在 $x = 2$ 处的切线方程为 $y = {6x} + b$ ，则 $a + b =$\blkda{$- {13}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            由题意知 $f\left( 2\right)  = \left( {2 - a}\right)  \times  \left( {2 - 2}\right)  \times  \left( {2 - 3}\right)  \times  \left( {2 - 4}\right)  = 0$ ,
            
            所以 $0 = 6 \times  2 + b$ ,解得 $b =  - {12}$ ,
            
            又 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \left( {x - a}\right) \left( {x - 3}\right) \left( {x - 4}\right)  + \left( {x - 2}\right) {\left\lbrack  \left( x - a\right) \left( x - 3\right) \left( x - 4\right) \right\rbrack  }^{\prime }$ ,
            
            所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 2\right)  = \left( {2 - a}\right)  \times  \left( {2 - 3}\right)  \times  \left( {2 - 4}\right)  = 6$ ,解得 $a =  - 1$ ,所以 $a + b =  - 1 + \left( {-{12}}\right)  =  - {13}$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $x =  - 1$ 处的切线方程为 $x + y - 1 = 0$ , 则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {-1}\right)  + f\left( {-1}\right)  =$ \blkda{1}
        \begin{jieda}
            由切线方程 $x + y - 1 = 0$ ,得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {-1}\right)  = k =  - 1$ ,
            
            将 $x =  - 1$ 代入切线方程 $x + y - 1 = 0$ ，得 $y = 2$ ，所以 $f\left( {-1}\right)  = 2$ ，
            
            则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {-1}\right)  + f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 1 + 2 = 1$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知直线 $y = {ax} - 1$ 与曲线 $y = \dfrac{\ln x}{x}$ 相切，则 $a$ 的值为\blkda{1}
        \begin{jieda}
            $y = \dfrac{\ln x}{x}$ 的导函数 ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1 - \ln x}{{x}^{2}}$ ,设切点为 $\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,则 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} {y}_{0} = a{x}_{0} - 1 \\  {y}_{0} = \dfrac{\ln {x}_{0}}{{x}_{0}} \\  a = \dfrac{1 - \ln {x}_{0}}{{x}_{0}^{2}} \end{matrix}\right.$ ,故 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} {y}_{0} = \dfrac{1 - \ln {x}_{0}}{{x}_{0}} - 1 \\  {y}_{0} = \dfrac{\ln {x}_{0}}{{x}_{0}} \end{matrix}\right.$ , 即 $\dfrac{\ln {x}_{0}}{{x}_{0}} = \dfrac{1 - \ln {x}_{0}}{{x}_{0}} - 1$ ，则 $2\ln {x}_{0} + {x}_{0} - 1 = 0$ .

            易得函数 $f\left( x\right)  = 2\ln x + x - 1$ 为增函数，且 $f\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ，故 ${x}_{0} = 1$ .故 $a = \dfrac{1 - \ln 1}{1} = 1$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 若直线 $y = \dfrac{1}{4}x + b$ 是曲线 $y = \ln x\left( {x > 0}\right)$ 的一条切线，则实数 $b$ 的值为\blkda{$ln4-1$}
        \begin{jieda}
            $\because y = \ln x$ 的导数 ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{x}$ , $\therefore$ 令 $\dfrac{1}{x} = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,得 $x = 4$ , $\therefore$ 切点为 $\left( {4,\ln 4}\right)$ .
            
            代入直线 $y = \dfrac{1}{4}x + b$ ，得 $\ln 4 = \dfrac{1}{4} \times  4 + b$ ，即 $b = \ln 4 - 1$ .
        \end{jieda}
        \item 若直线 $y = {2x}$ 是曲线 $f\left( x\right)  = x\left( {{{e}}^{2x} - a}\right)$ 的切线，则 $a =$\blkda{$-1$}
        \begin{jieda}
             $f\left( x\right)  = x\left( {{{e}}^{2x} - a}\right) ,f'\left( x\right)  = \left( {1 + {2x}}\right) {{e}}^{2x} - a$ ,
            
            依题意,直线 $y = {2x}$ 是曲线 $f\left( x\right)  = x\left( {{{e}}^{2x} - a}\right)$ 的切线,
            
            设切点为 $\left( {t,{2t}}\right)$ ,则 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} t\left( {{{e}}^{2t} - a}\right)  = {2t} \\  \left( {1 + {2t}}\right) {{e}}^{2t} - a = 2 \end{matrix}\right.$ , $\left\{  \begin{matrix} t{{e}}^{2t} = \left( {2 + a}\right) t \\  \left( {1 + {2t}}\right) {{e}}^{2t} = 2 + a \end{matrix}\right.$ ,
            
            通过对比系数可得 $\left( {1 + {2t}}\right) t = t,2{t}^{2} = 0,t = 0$ ,则 $a =  - 1$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} + a\ln x$ ，在点 $\left( {1,f\left( 1\right) }\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $x - y = 0$ ,则 $a =$\blkda{2}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $f\left( x\right)  = \frac{1}{x} + a\ln x$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{a}{x} - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}$
            
            因为函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {1,f\left( 1\right) }\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $x - y = 0$ ,则 $f\left( 1\right)  = a - 1 = 1$ ,解得 $a = 2$ ,
            
            且 $f\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,则点 $\left( {1,f\left( 1\right) }\right)$ 在直线 $x - y = 0$ 上,合乎题意.
            
            因此, $a = 2$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
    \end{enumerate}
    \item 两条切线平行、垂直、重合 (公切线) 问题
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 若曲线 $y = {x}^{2} - a\ln x$ 在点 $P\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线与直线 $y = x - 2$ 垂直, 则实数 $a$ 的值为\blkda{3}
        \begin{jieda}
            由 $y = {x}^{2} - a\ln x$ ,求导 ${y}^{\prime } = {2x} - \dfrac{a}{x}$ ,
            
            则 $y = {x}^{2} - a\ln x$ 在点 $P\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线的斜率为 ${\left. {y}^{\prime }\right| }_{x = 1} = 2 - a$ ,
            
            而 $y = {x}^{2} - a\ln x$ 在点 $P\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线与直线 $y = x - 2$ 垂直，
            
            则 $2 - a =  - 1$ ，故 $a = 3$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 若直线 $x + y + a = 0$ 是曲线 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} + {bx} - {14}$ 与曲线 $g\left( x\right)  =  {x}^{2} - {3\ln }x$ 的公切线，则 $a - b =$\blkda{11}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $g\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - 3\ln x$，所以 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} - \dfrac{3}{x}$ ,由 ${2x} - \dfrac{3}{x} =  - 1$ ,解得 $x = 1$ 或 $x =  - \dfrac{3}{2}$ (舍去)，所以切点为 $\left( {1,1}\right)$
            
            因为切点在切线 $x + y + a = 0$ 上,解得 $a =  - 2$，所以切线方程为 $x + y - 2 = 0$
            
            ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} + b$ 设切点为 $\left( {t,{t}^{3} + {bt} - {14}}\right)$ ,
            
            由题意得 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} 3{t}^{2} + b =  - 1 \\  t + {t}^{3} + {bt} - {14} - 2 = 0 \end{matrix}\right.$ ,解得 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} b =  - {13} \\  t =  - 2 \end{matrix}\right.$ ,
            
            所以 $a - b = {11}$
        \end{jieda}

        
        \item 已知 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} + {nx} - {52},g\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - 3\ln x$ ，若直线 $x + y + m = 0$ 是曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 与曲线 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的公切线,则 $m - n =$\blkda{26}
        \begin{jieda}
            设直线 $x + y + m = 0$ 与曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 相切于点 $\left( {a, - a - m}\right)$ ,与曲线 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 相切于点 $\left( {b, - b - m}\right) ,b > 0$ .
            
            由 $g\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - 3\ln x$ 知 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} - \dfrac{3}{x}$ ,又两曲线的公切线斜率为 -1,则 ${2b} - \dfrac{3}{b} =  - 1$ ,解得 $b = 1$ 或 $b =  - \dfrac{3}{2}$ (舍去).
            
            所以 $1 - 3\ln 1 =  - 1 - m$ ,解得 $m =  - 2$ .
            
            由 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} + {nx} - {52}$ 知 $f\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} + n$ ，又两曲线的公切线斜率为 -1，则 $3{a}^{2} + n =  - 1$ ，即 $n =  - 3{a}^{2} - 1$ ，故 ${a}^{3} - \left( {3{a}^{2} + 1}\right) a - {52} =  - a + 2$ ,整理得 ${a}^{3} =  - {27}$ ,故 $a =  - 3$ ,
            
            所以 $n =  - 3{a}^{2} - 1 =  - {28}$ ,故 $m - n = {26}$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  - {x}^{3} + x + 1,g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-{2x} + 1}$ .
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 求曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 1$ 处的切线方程
            \item 若曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 1$ 处的切线与曲线 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = t\left( {t \in  {R}}\right)$ 处的切线平行,求 $t$ 的值
        \end{enumerate}
        \begin{jieda}
            \begin{enumerate}
                \item ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - 3{x}^{2} + 1,f\left( 1\right)  = 1,{f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  =  - 2$ ,

                所以曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = 1$ 处的切线方程为 $y - 1 =  - 2\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,即 ${2x} + y - 3 = 0$

                \item 由(1)可得:曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y =  - {2x} + 3$ 
                
                由 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - 2{{e}}^{-{2x} + 1}$ ,可得曲线 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = t\left( {t \in  {R}}\right)$ 处的切线斜率为 ${g}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  =  - 2{{e}}^{-{2t} + 1}$，由题意可得 $- 2{{e}}^{-{2t} + 1} =  - 2$ ，从而 $t = \dfrac{1}{2}$
            \end{enumerate}
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 若曲线 $y = \sqrt{x}$ 在点 $\left( {4,2}\right)$ 处的切线也是曲线 $y = a{{e}}^{x}$ 的切线,则 $a =$\blkda{$\dfrac{{{e}}^{3}}{4}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            $y = \sqrt{x}$ 的导数 ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{x}}$ ,令 $x = 4$ ,则 ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,

            所以曲线 $y = \sqrt{x}$ 在 $\left( {4,2}\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y - 2 = \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {x - 4}\right)$，即 $x - {4y} + 4 = 0$ .
            
            $y = a{{e}}^{x}$ 的导数 ${y}^{\prime } = a{{e}}^{x}$ ,设直线 $x - {4y} + 4 = 0$ 与曲线 $y = a{{e}}^{x}$ 切于点 $\left( {{x}_{0},a{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}}\right)$ ,
            
            则曲线 $y = a{{e}}^{x}$ 在点 $\left( {{x}_{0},a{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}}\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y - a{{e}}^{{x}_{0}} = a{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}\left( {x - {x}_{0}}\right)$ ,
            
            即 $x - \dfrac{1}{a{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}}y + 1 - {x}_{0} = 0$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{1}{a{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}} = 4, \\  1 - {x}_{0} = 4, \end{array}\right.$ 解得 $a = \dfrac{{{e}}^{3}}{4}$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {e}^{x - a},g\left( x\right)  = \ln x - b$ .
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 当 $a = 1$ 时,求曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线方程
            \item 若 $a = b + 2$ ，是否存在直线与曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 和 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 都相切？若存在，求出所有这样的直线；若不存在, 请说明理由.
        \end{enumerate}
        
        \begin{jieda}
            \begin{enumerate}
                \item 当 $a = 1$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {e}^{x - 1},f\left( 1\right)  = 1,{f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = 1$
                
                曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线方程为: $y - f\left( 1\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right) \left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,
                
                代入整理得: $y = x$ .
                \item 设直线与曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 相切于点 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ,与曲线 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 相切于点 $B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) ,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {e}^{x - a}$ , ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x}$
                
                曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $A$ 处的切线为: $y - {e}^{{x}_{1} - a} = {e}^{{x}_{1} - a}\left( {x - {x}_{1}}\right)$
                
                与曲线 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 相切于点 $B$ ,则 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} {e}^{{x}_{1} - a} = \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}} \\  \ln {x}_{2} - b - {e}^{{x}_{1} - a} = {e}^{{x}_{1} - a}\left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}\right) \end{matrix}\right.$
                
                由上式得: ${x}_{1} - a = \ln \left(\dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}}\right) =  - \ln {x}_{2}$ ，则 $\ln {x}_{2} = a - {x}_{1}$
                
                将 ${e}^{{x}_{1} - a} = \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}}\text{ 、 }\ln {x}_{2} = a - {x}_{1}$ 代入下式得: $a - {x}_{1} - b - \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}}\left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}\right)$ ，
                
                整理得: $\left( {{x}_{1} - 1}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} - 1}\right)  = 0$

                \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                    \item 当 ${x}_{1} = 1$ 时， $y - {e}^{1 - a} = {e}^{1 - a}\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ，即切线为 $y = {e}^{1 - a}x$
                    \item 当 ${x}_{2} = 1$ 时， $a - {x}_{1} = \ln {x}_{2} = 0,{x}_{1} = a$ ，因此切线为 $y - 1 = x - a$
                \end{dingautolist}
            \end{enumerate}
        \end{jieda}
    \end{enumerate}

    \item 曲线上两点间距离最值问题
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 已知实数 ${a}$ 、 ${b}$ 、 ${c}$ 、 ${d}$ 满足 $\left| {{b} - \dfrac{{{lna}} }{{a}}}\right|  + \left| {{c} - {d} + 2}\right|  = 0$ ,则 ${\left( {a} - {c}\right) }^{2} + {\left( {b} - {d}\right) }^{2}$ 的最小值为\blkda{$\dfrac{9}{2}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            $\left| {{b} - \dfrac{ {{lna}} }{{a}}}\right|  + \left| {{c} - {d} + 2}\right|  = 0 \Longleftrightarrow \left\{\begin{array}{l}
                 b = \dfrac{lna}{a}  \\
                 d = c+2
            \end{array}\right.$

            ${\left( {a} - {c}\right) }^{2} + {\left( {b} - {d}\right) }^{2}$即$Q(a, b)$与$P(c, d)$两点距离平方，$Q$在$y = \dfrac{lnx}{x}$上，$P$在$y = x+2$上.

            \begin{center}
                \includegraphics[width=0.25\linewidth]{bodyxb//src/quxianshangliangdianjulizuizhi1.png}
            \end{center}

            对于任意的$Q$，过$Q$向直线做垂线，垂足取$P$取得最小值，$PQ$长度即$Q$到直线距离. 计算可知$x = 1$处$y = \dfrac{lnx}{x}$的切线与$y = x+2$平行，此时取得$Q$到直线距离的最小值，此时目标值为$\dfrac{9}{2}$
            
        \end{jieda}

        \item 对于函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ ,记 ${h}^{\left( 0\right) }\left( x\right)  = h\left( x\right) ,{h}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( x\right)  = {\left( h\left( x\right) \right) }^{\prime },\cdots ,{h}^{\left( n + 1\right) }\left( x\right)  = {\left( {h}^{\left( n\right) }\left( x\right) \right) }^{\prime }\left( {n \in  \mathbf{N}}\right)$ . 如果 $n$ 是满足 ${h}^{\left( n\right) }\left( x\right)  = h\left( x\right)$ 的最小正整数,则称 $n$ 是函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 的“最小导周期”.
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 已知 $f\left( x\right)  = a\sin \left( {x + t}\right)  + b\cos \left( {x + t}\right)$ ,证明：对任意 $a,b,t \in  {R},f\left( x\right)$ 的最小导周期为 4
            \item 设 $m,n \in  {R},g\left( x\right)  = {e}^{mx} + n\cos x$ ,若函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的最小导周期为 2 ,记$M\left( {a,b}\right)  = \sqrt{{\left( a - b\right) }^{2} + {\left( a + 1 + g\left( b\right) \right) }^{2}}$ ,当实数 $a,b$ 变化时,求 $M\left( {a,b}\right)$ 的最小值
            \item 设 $\omega  > 1,h\left( x\right)  = \cos {\omega x}$ ,若函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 满足 ${h}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( x\right)  \leq  x$ 对 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 恒成立,且存在${x}_{0} \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 使得 ${{h}}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = {x}_{0}$ ,试用 $\omega$ 表示 ${x}_{0}$ ,并证明 $\dfrac{\pi }{2\omega } < {x}_{0} < \dfrac{\pi }{\omega }$ .
        \end{enumerate}
        \begin{jieda}
            \begin{enumerate}
                \item 证明: 因为 ${f}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( x\right)  = a\cos \left( {x + t}\right)  - b\sin \left( {x + t}\right)$ ,${f}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( x\right)  =  - a\sin \left( {x + t}\right)  - b\cos \left( {x + t}\right) , {f}^{\left( 3\right) }\left( x\right)  =  - a\cos \left( {x + t}\right)  + b\sin \left( {x + t}\right) , {f}^{\left( 4\right) }\left( x\right)  = a\sin \left( {x + t}\right)  + b\cos \left( {x + t}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$
                
                所以,对任意实数 $a,b,t$ ,都有 ${f}^{\left( 4\right) }\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,即最小导周期为 4 .
                
                \item $g\left( x\right)  = {g}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( x\right)$ ,则 ${{e}}^{mx} + n\cos x = {m}^{2}{{e}}^{mx} - n\cos x$ 对任意实数 $x$ 恒成立
                
                令 $x = 0$ ,则 $1 + n = {m}^{2} - n$ ,即 ${m}^{2} = 1 + {2n}$ 
                
                令 $x = \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,则 ${{e}}^{\frac{m\pi }{2}} = {m}^{2}{{e}}^{\frac{m\pi }{2}}$，则 ${m}^{2} = 1$ 

                所以 $m = 1,n = 0$ (舍,由于 ${g}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} = g\left( x\right)$ ,最小导周期不是 2 ) 或 $m =  - 1,n = 0$ .故 $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-x}$ .

                $M\left( {a,b}\right)  = \sqrt{{\left( a - b\right) }^{2} + {\left( a + 1 + g\left( b\right) \right) }^{2}} = \sqrt{{\left( a - b\right) }^{2} + {\left( -a - 1 - {{e}}^{-b}\right) }^{2}}$ 的最小值可视为点$P\left( {a, - a - 1}\right)$ 与点 $Q\left( {b,{{e}}^{-b}}\right)$ 之间的最短距离,即曲线 $y = {{e}}^{-x}$ 上的点到直线 $y =  - x - 1$距离的最小值.
                \begin{center}
                    \includegraphics[width=0.25\linewidth]{bodyxb//src/quxianshangliangdianjulizuizhi2.png}
                \end{center}
                设切点 $M\left( {{x}_{0},{{e}}^{-{x}_{0}}}\right) ,{y}^{\prime } =  - {{e}}^{-x}$ ,切线斜率 $- {{e}}^{-{x}_{0}} =  - 1$ ,得 ${x}_{0} = 0$ ,切点为(0,1)，点(0,1)到直线 $y =  - x - 1$ 距离 $d = \dfrac{\left| 1 + 0 + 1\right| }{\sqrt{{1}^{2} + {1}^{2}}} = \sqrt{2}$ . 即 $M\left( {a,b}\right)$ 的最小值为 $\sqrt{2}$ .

                \item $h^{(2)}(x) = -\omega^2cos\omega x$，设$\varphi(x) = -\omega^2cos\omega x - x$，则$x = x_0$是$\varphi(x)$的极大值点，$\varphi'(x) = \omega^3sin\omega x - 1$，故有$\left\{\begin{array}{l}
                     sin\omega x_0 = \dfrac{1}{\omega^3} \\
                     cos\omega x_0 = -\dfrac{x_0}{\omega^2}
                \end{array}\right.$，因此有$1 = \dfrac{1}{\omega^6} + \dfrac{x_0^2}{\omega^4}$，因此$x_0 = \dfrac{\sqrt{\omega^6-1}}{\omega}$

                $\left\{\begin{array}{l}
                     sin\omega x_0 > 0 \\
                     cos\omega x_0 < 0
                \end{array}\right.$，故$\omega x_0 \in \left(2k\pi +\dfrac{1}{2}\pi, 2k\pi+\pi\right), k \in \mathbf{Z}$，由$\omega x_0 > 0$可知$k \geq 0$.

                假设$k > 0$，则$\varphi\left(x_0 - \dfrac{2k\pi}{\omega}\right) = -\omega^2cos\omega \left(x_0 - \dfrac{2k\pi}{\omega}\right) - \left(x_0 - \dfrac{2k\pi}{\omega}\right) = -\omega^2cos\omega x_0 - \left(x_0 - \dfrac{2k\pi}{\omega}\right) = \dfrac{2k\pi}{\omega} > 0$，与$\varphi(x) \leq 0$矛盾.

                故$k = 0$，$\omega x_0 \in \left(\dfrac{1}{2}\pi, \pi\right)$，即$\dfrac{\pi }{2\omega } < {x}_{0} < \dfrac{\pi }{\omega }$.
            \end{enumerate}
        \end{jieda}
    \end{enumerate}

    \item 导数与新定义
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 给出定义:若函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $D$ 上可导，即 $f'\left( x\right)$ 存在，且导函数 $f'\left( x\right)$ 在 $D$ 上也可导,则称 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $D$ 上存在二阶导数,记 $f''\left( x\right)  = {\left( f'\left( x\right) \right) }^{\prime }$ . 若 $f''\left( x\right)  < 0$ 在 $D$ 上恒成立,则称 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $D$ 上为凸函数. 以下四个函数在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{{\pi }}{2}}\right)$ 上不是是凸函数的是\dotfill{(\kh{D})}
        \xx{$f\left( x\right)  = \sin x + \cos x$}{$f\left( x\right)  = \ln x - {2x}$}{$f\left( x\right)  =  - {x}^{3} + {2x} - 1$}{$f\left( x\right)  =  - x{{e}}^{-x}$}
        
        \begin{jieda}
            对于 ${A} : f\left( x\right)  = \sin x + \cos x,f'\left( x\right)  = \cos x - \sin x,f''\left( x\right)  =  - \sin x - \cos x$ ,
            
            则 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{{\pi }}{2}}\right)$ 上恒有 ${f}''\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,故 ${A}$ 错误;
            
            对于 ${B} : f\left( x\right)  = \ln x - {2x},f'\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - 2,{f}''\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}$ ,
            
            则 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 上恒有 ${f}''\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,故 ${B}$ 错误;
            
            对于 ${C} : f\left( x\right)  =  - {x}^{3} + {2x} - 1,f'\left( x\right)  =  - 3{x}^{2} + 2,{f}''\left( x\right)  =  - {6x}$ ,
            
            则 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{{\pi }}{2}}\right)$ 上恒有 ${f}''\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,故 ${C}$ 错误;
            
            对于 ${D} : f\left( x\right)  =  - x{{e}}^{-x},f'\left( x\right)  =  - {{e}}^{-x} + x{{e}}^{-x},f''\left( x\right)  = \left( {2 - x}\right) {{e}}^{-x}$ ,
            
            则 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{{\pi }}{2}}\right)$ 上恒有 ${f}''\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,故 ${D}$ 正确.
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 曲率是刻画曲线弯曲程度的重要指标，曲线的曲率定义如下: 记 $f'\left( x\right)$ 是 $f\left( x\right)$ 的导函数， $f''\left( x\right)$ 是 $f'\left( x\right)$ 的导函数，那么曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {{x}_{0},f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }\right)$ 处的曲率 $K = \dfrac{\left| f''\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right| }{{\left\{  1 + {\left\lbrack  f'\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}\right\}  }^{\frac{3}{2}}}$ ， 则曲线 $f\left( x\right)  = \sin x + \cos x$ 在点 $\left( {\dfrac{\pi }{4},\sqrt{2}}\right)$ 处的曲率为\blkda{$\sqrt{2}$}
        \begin{jieda}
            对函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \sin x + \cos x$ 求导,得 $f'\left( x\right)  = \cos x - \sin x$ 
            
            对 $f'\left( x\right)$ 求导，得 $f''\left( x\right)  =  - \sin x - \cos x$ ，所以 $f'\left( \dfrac{\pi }{4}\right)  = \cos \dfrac{\pi }{4} - \sin \dfrac{\pi }{4} = 0$ ， $f''\left( \dfrac{\pi }{4}\right)  =  - \sin \dfrac{\pi }{4} - \cos \dfrac{\pi }{4} =  - \sqrt{2}$
            
            所以曲线 $f\left( x\right)  = \sin x + \cos x$ 在点 $\left( {\dfrac{\pi }{4},\sqrt{2}}\right)$ 处的曲率 $K = \dfrac{\left| f''\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right| }{{\left\{  1 + {\left\lbrack  f'\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}\right\}  }^{\frac{3}{2}}} = \dfrac{\left| -\sqrt{2}\right| }{{\left( 1 + {0}^{2}\right) }^{\frac{3}{2}}} = \sqrt{2}$ .
        \end{jieda}

        \item 对于三次函数 $f\left( x\right)  = a{x}^{3} + b{x}^{2} + {cx} + d\left( {a \neq  0}\right)$ ，定义:设 ${f}''\left( x\right)$ 是函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的导函数 $y = f'\left( x\right)$ 的导数，若 ${f}''\left( x\right)  = 0$ 有实数解 ${x}_{0}$ ，则称点 $\left( {{x}_{0},f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }\right)$ 为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的“拐点”. 现已知 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} - 3{x}^{2} + {2x} - 2$ . 请解答下列问题:
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 求函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的“拐点” $A$ 的坐标
            \item 求证: $f\left( x\right)$ 的图像关于“拐点” $A$ 对称，并求 $f\left( {-{2020}}\right)  + f\left( {-{2019}}\right)  + \cdots f\left( {2019}\right)  + f\left( {2022}\right)$ 的值.
        \end{enumerate}
        \begin{jieda}
            \begin{enumerate}
                \item $\because f'\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} - {6x} + 2,f''\left( x\right)  = {6x} - 6,\therefore$ 令 $f''\left( x\right)  = {6x} - 6 = 0$，得 $x = 1$ .
                
                有 $f\left( 1\right)  = {1}^{3} - 3 + 2 - 2 =  - 2,\therefore$ “拐点” $A$ 为 $\left( {1, - 2}\right)$ .

                \item 证明:设 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 是 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 图像上任意一点，则 ${y}_{0} = {x}_{0}^{3} - 3{x}_{0}^{2} + 2{x}_{0} - 2$ .
                
                $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 是关于“拐点” $A\left( {1, - 2}\right)$ 的对称点为 $P\left( {2 - {x}_{0}, - 4 - {y}_{0}}\right)$ .
                
                把点 $P$ 坐标代入 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 得左边 $=  - 4 - {y}_{0} =  - {x}_{0}^{3} + 3{x}_{0}^{2} - 2{x}_{0} - 2$ ,
                
                右边 $= {\left( 2 - {x}_{0}\right) }^{3} - 3{\left( 2 - {x}_{0}\right) }^{2} + 2\left( {2 - {x}_{0}}\right)  - 2 =  - {x}_{0}^{3} + 3{x}_{0}^{2} - 2{x}_{0} - 2,\therefore$ 左边 $=$ 右边.
                
                $\therefore$ 点 $P\left( {2 - {x}_{0}, - 4 - {y}_{0}}\right)$ 在 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像上.
                
                $\therefore y = f\left( x\right)$ 关于“拐点” $A$ 对称.
                
                由对称性可得 $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {2 - x}\right)  =  - 4$
                
                $f\left( {-{2020}}\right)  + f\left( {-{2019}}\right)  + \cdots f\left( {2019}\right)  + f\left( {2022}\right)  = {2021} \times  \left( {-4}\right)  + f\left( 1\right)  =  - {8086}.$
            \end{enumerate}
        \end{jieda}

        

        \item 给出定义: 设 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 是函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的导函数, ${f}^{\prime \prime }\left( x\right)$ 是函数 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 的导函数,若方程 ${f}^{\prime \prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ 有实数解 $x = {x}_{0}$ ,则称 $\left( {{x}_{0},f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }\right)$ 为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的 “拐点”. 经研究发现所有的三次函数 $f\left( x\right)  = a{x}^{3} + b{x}^{2} + {cx} + d\left( {a \neq  0}\right)$ 都有“拐点”,且该“拐点”也是函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像的对称中心. 若函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  - {x}^{3} + 3{x}^{2}$ , 则 $f\left( \dfrac{1}{2021}\right)  + f\left( \dfrac{2}{2021}\right)  + f\left( \dfrac{3}{2021}\right)  + \cdots  + f\left( \dfrac{4040}{2021}\right)  + f\left( \dfrac{4041}{2021}\right)  =$\blkda{8082}
        
        \item 给出以下三个材料:
        \begin{dingautolist}{172}
            \item 若函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 可导,我们通常把导函数 $f'\left( x\right)$ 的导数叫做 $f\left( x\right)$ 的二阶导数，记作 $f''\left( x\right)$ . 类似的，函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的二阶导数的导数叫做函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的三阶导数，记作 ${f}^{\prime \prime }\left( x\right)$ ，函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的三阶导数的导数叫做函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的四阶导数......,一般地,函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的 $n - 1$ 阶导数的导数叫做函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的 $n$ 阶导数,记作 ${f}^{\left( n\right) }\left( x\right)  = {\left\lbrack  {f}^{n - 1}\left( x\right) \right\rbrack  }^{\prime },n \geq  4$
            \item 若 $n \in  {{N}}^{ * }$ ，定义 $n! = n \times  \left( {n - 1}\right)  \times  \left( {n - 2}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  3 \times  2 \times  1$
            \item 若函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在包含 ${x}_{0}$ 的某个开区间 $\left( {a,b}\right)$ 上具有任意阶的导数，那么对于任意 $x \in  \left( {a,b}\right)$ 有 $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + \dfrac{f'\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{1!}\left( {x - {x}_{0}}\right)  + \dfrac{f''\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{2!}{\left( x - {x}_{0}\right) }^{2} + \cdots  + \dfrac{{f}^{\left( n\right) }\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{n!}{\left( x - {x}_{0}\right) }^{n} + \cdots$ ,我们将 $g\left( x\right)$ 称为函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $x = {x}_{0}$ 处的泰勒展开式.

            例如 ${f}_{1}\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x}$ 在点 $x = 0$ 处的泰勒展开式为 ${g}_{1}\left( x\right)  = 1 + x + \dfrac{1}{2}{x}^{2} + \cdots  + \dfrac{1}{n!}{x}^{n} + \cdots$
        \end{dingautolist}
        
        根据以上三段材料，完成下面的题目:
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 求出 $f\left( x\right)  = \cos x$ 在点 $x = 0$ 处的泰勒展开式 $g\left( x\right)$
            \item 用 $f\left( x\right)  = \cos x$ 在点 $x = 0$ 处的泰勒展开式前三项计算 $\cos {0.3}$ 的值，精确到小数点后 4 位.
            \item 现已知 $\dfrac{\sin x}{x} = \left( {1 - \dfrac{x}{\pi }}\right) \left( {1 + \dfrac{x}{\pi }}\right) \left( {1 - \dfrac{x}{2\pi }}\right) \left( {1 + \dfrac{x}{2\pi }}\right) \cdots \left( {1 - \dfrac{x}{n \pi }}\right) \left( {1 + \dfrac{x}{n \pi }}\right) \cdots$ ，试求 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{n = 1}}^{\infty }\dfrac{1}{{n}^{2}}$ 的值
        \end{enumerate}
        \begin{jieda}
            \begin{enumerate}
                \item $f\left( x\right)  = \cos x, f'\left( x\right)  =  - \sin x,{f}''\left( x\right)  =  - \cos x,\cdots$ ，
                
                所以 $f\left( 0\right)  = \cos 0 = 1,f'\left( 0\right)  =  - \sin 0 = 0,{f}''\left( x\right)  =  - \cos 0 =  - 1,\cdots$ ,
                
                由 $\cos x = 1 + \dfrac{0}{1!}\left( {x - 0}\right)  + \dfrac{-1}{2!}{\left( x - 0\right) }^{2} + \cdots  + \dfrac{{\left( -1\right) }^{n}}{n!}{\left( x - 0\right) }^{2n} + \cdots$
                
                所以 $\cos x = 1 - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2!} + \dfrac{{x}^{4}}{4!} - \dfrac{{x}^{6}}{6!} + \cdots  + \dfrac{{\left( -1\right) }^{n}{x}^{2n}}{\left( {2n}\right) !} + \cdots$

                \item 由(1)可得 $\cos {0.3} = 1 - \dfrac{{0.3}^{2}}{2!} + \dfrac{{0.3}^{4}}{4!} - \dfrac{{0.3}^{6}}{6!} + \cdots  + \dfrac{{\left( -1\right) }^{n} \times  {0.3}^{2n}}{\left( {2n}\right) !} + \cdots  \approx  1 - \dfrac{{0.3}^{2}}{2!} + \dfrac{{0.3}^{4}}{4!}  = 1 - {0.045} + {0.0003375} = {0.9553}$

                \item 因为 $\dfrac{\sin x}{x} = \left( {1 - \dfrac{x}{\pi}}\right) \left( {1 + \dfrac{x}{\pi}}\right) \left( {1 - \dfrac{x}{2\pi}}\right) \left( {1 + \dfrac{x}{2\pi}}\right) \cdots \left( {1 - \dfrac{x}{n\pi}}\right) \left( {1 + \dfrac{x}{n\pi}}\right) \cdots  = \left( {1 - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{{\pi}}^{2}}}\right) \left( {1 - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4{{\pi}}^{2}}}\right) \cdots \left( {1 - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{n}^{2}{{\pi}}^{2}}}\right)$
                
                对 $\cos x = 1 - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2!} + \dfrac{{x}^{4}}{4!} - \dfrac{{x}^{6}}{6!} + \cdots  + \dfrac{{\left( -1\right) }^{n}{x}^{2n}}{\left( {2n}\right) !} + \cdots$ ,
                
                两边求导可得: $- \sin x =  - \dfrac{x}{1!} + \dfrac{{x}^{3}}{3!} - \dfrac{{x}^{5}}{5!} + \cdots  + \dfrac{{\left( -1\right) }^{n}{x}^{{2n} - 1}}{\left( {{2n} - 1}\right) !} + \cdots$ ,
                
                所以 $\sin x = \dfrac{x}{1!} - \dfrac{{x}^{3}}{3!} + \dfrac{{x}^{5}}{5!} + \cdots  + \dfrac{{\left( -1\right) }^{n - 1}{x}^{{2n} - 1}}{\left( {{2n} - 1}\right) !} + \cdots$ ,
                
                所以 $\dfrac{\sin x}{x} = 1 - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{3!} + \dfrac{{x}^{4}}{5!} + \cdots  + \dfrac{{\left( -1\right) }^{n - 1}{x}^{{2n} - 2}}{\left( {{2n} - 1}\right) !}$
                
                比较两式中 ${x}^{2}$ 的系数，可得：$- \dfrac{1}{3!} =  - \dfrac{1}{{\pi }^{2}}\left( {\dfrac{1}{{1}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{{2}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{{3}^{2}}\cdots  + \dfrac{1}{{n}^{2}} + \cdots }\right)$
                
                所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{n = 1}}^{\infty }\dfrac{1}{{n}^{2}} = \dfrac{1}{{1}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{{2}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{{3}^{2}}\cdots  + \dfrac{1}{{n}^{2}} + \cdots  = \dfrac{{\pi}^{2}}{6}$ .
            \end{enumerate}
        \end{jieda}
    \end{enumerate}

    \item 利用导数判断单调性、求单调区间
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x + \dfrac{{{e}}^{x} + 1}{x}$ 的单调增区间为\blkda{$\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$}
        \begin{jieda}
            由函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x + \dfrac{{{e}}^{x} + 1}{x}$ ,可得其定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$，且 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} + \dfrac{x{{e}}^{x} - \left( {{{e}}^{x} + 1}\right) }{{x}^{2}} = \dfrac{\left( {x - 1}\right) \left( {{{e}}^{x} + 1}\right) }{{x}^{2}}$，令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,解得 $x > 1$ ,所以函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的单调增区间为 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln \left( {\cos x}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{2}\sin {2x} - x$ ，则\dotfill{(\kh{B})}
        \xx{$f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 内单调递增}{$f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 内单调递减}{$f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)$ 内单调递增，在区间 $\left( {\dfrac{\pi }{4},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 内单调递减}{$f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)$ 内单调递减，在区间 $\left( {\dfrac{\pi }{4},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 内单调递增}
        
        \begin{jieda}
            由题意 $\cos x > 0,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{\sin x}{\cos x} + \cos {2x} - 1 =  - \left( {\tan x + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{1 - {\tan }^{2}x}{1 + {\tan }^{2}x} = \dfrac{-\tan x{\left( \tan x + 1\right) }^{2}}{{\tan }^{2}x + 1}$
            
            当 $x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{\pi}{2}}\right)$ 时， $\tan x > 0$ ，则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ，所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 上单调递减.
        \end{jieda}
        

        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + 2{f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right) x - \cos x$ .
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 求 $f\left( x\right)$ 的解析式
            \item 判断 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ 上的单调性.
        \end{enumerate}
        \begin{jieda}
            \begin{enumerate}
                \item 因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + 2{f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right) x - \cos x$ ，所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + 2{f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  + \sin x$ ，
                
                则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = {{e}}^{0} + 2{f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  + \sin 0 = 1 + 2{f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)$ ，所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  =  - 1$ ，
                
                所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - {2x} - \cos x$ .
                \item ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - 2 + \sin x$ ,
                
                当 $x \leq  0$ 时， $0 < {{e}}^{x} \leq  1,\sin x \leq  1$ ，
                
                所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - 2 + \sin x \leq  0$ 恒成立,
                
                所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $( - \infty ,0\rbrack$ 上的单调递减.
            \end{enumerate}
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - \left( {{2a} + 1}\right) x + a\ln x + a$ .
        \begin{enumerate}
            \item 当 $a = 1$ 时，求函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的单调区间
            \item 当 $a \in  {R}$ 时,求 $f\left( x\right)$ 的单调区间
        \end{enumerate}
        \begin{jieda}
            \begin{enumerate}
                \item 当 $a = 1$ 时， $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - {3x} + \ln x + 1$ ，定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ，
                
                ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} - 3 + \dfrac{1}{x} = \dfrac{\left( {{2x} - 1}\right) \left( {x - 1}\right) }{x},$
                
                令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,得 $x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ ,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,得 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{1}{2},1}\right)$ ,
                
                所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 的单调递增区间为 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right) ,\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ ,单调递减区间为 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{2},1}\right)$ .

                \item $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - \left( {{2a} + 1}\right) x + a\ln x + a$ ，定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ，
                
                ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} - \left( {{2a} + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{a}{x} = \dfrac{\left( {{2x} - 1}\right) \left( {x - a}\right) }{x}$ ,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ ,得 $x = \dfrac{1}{2}$ 或 $x = a$ .

                \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                    \item 当 $a \leq  0$ 时，当 $x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,f\left( x\right)$ 单调递减，
                    
                    当 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{1}{2}, + \infty }\right)$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,f\left( x\right)$ 单调递增
                    \item 当 $0 < a < \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时，当 $x \in  \left( {0,a}\right)$ 和 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{1}{2}, + \infty }\right)$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,f\left( x\right)$ 单调递增，
                    
                    当 $x \in  \left( {a,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,f\left( x\right)$ 单调递减

                    \item 当 $a = \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ 对 $\forall x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 恒成立，所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 单调递增

                    \item 当 $a > \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时，当 $x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ 和 $x \in  \left( {a, + \infty }\right)$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,f\left( x\right)$ 单调递增，
                
                    当 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{1}{2},a}\right)$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,f\left( x\right)$ 单调递减
                \end{dingautolist}
            \end{enumerate}
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 设函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ ,其中 $f\left( x\right)  = {kx} + \sin x,k \geq  1,k \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,定义域为 $D = \left( {-\infty , + \infty }\right)$ . 对于 $t \in  D$ ,定义${S}_{f\left( t\right) } = \{ x \mid  f\left( x\right)  \leq  f\left( t\right) \}$ ,则 ${S}_{f\left( {2025}\right) } =$\blkda{$(-\infty, 2025]$}
        \begin{jieda}
            $f'(x) = k + cosx \geq 0$，因此$f(x)$严增，故${S}_{f\left( t\right) } = \{ x \mid  f\left( x\right)  \leq  f\left( t\right) \} = (0, t]$.
        \end{jieda}
    
    \end{enumerate}


    \item 根据单调性解不等式
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 定义在 $(-\infty, 0) \cup \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$的函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的导函数为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,若 $x{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  + f\left( x\right) = e^x$ ,且 $f\left( 2\right)  = 0$ ,则不等式 $\left( {x - 2}\right) f\left( x\right)  < 0$ 的解集为\blkda{$\left(-\infty, 0\right)$}
        \begin{jieda}
            $x{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  + f\left( x\right) = e^x \Longleftrightarrow \left[xf(x)\right]' = e^x$，则$f(x) = \dfrac{e^x+c}{x}$，$f(2) = 0$则$f(x) = \dfrac{e^x-e^2}{x}$

            \begin{dingautolist}{172}
                \item 当$x > 2$时，$f(x) > 0, x-2 > 0$，故不满足
                \item 当$x \in (0, 2)$时，$f(x) < 0, x-2 < 0$，故不满足
                \item 当$x < 0$时，$f(x) > 0, x-2 < 0$，故满足.
            \end{dingautolist}
        \end{jieda}
        \item 定义在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上的函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的导函数为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,若 $x{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - f\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,且 $f\left( 3\right)  = 0$ ,则不等式 $\left( {x - 2}\right) f\left( x\right)  < 0$ 的解集为\blkda[3]{$\left( {0,2}\right)  \cup  \left( {3, + \infty }\right)$}
        \begin{jieda}
            依题意,令 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{x}$ ,求导得 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - f\left( x\right) }{{x}^{2}} < 0$ ,则 $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递减, 由 $f\left( 3\right)  = 0$ ,得 $g\left( 3\right)  = 0$ ,不等式 $\left( {x - 2}\right) f\left( x\right)  < 0 \Longleftrightarrow  \left( {x - 2}\right)  \cdot  \dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{x} < 0 \Longleftrightarrow  \left( {x - 2}\right) g\left( x\right)  < 0$ , 则 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x - 2 < 0 \\  g\left( x\right)  > 0 \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x - 2 > 0 \\  g\left( x\right)  < 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,即 $\left\{  \begin{matrix} x < 2 \\  0 < x < 3 \end{matrix}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x > 2 \\  x > 3 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $0 < x < 2$ 或 $x > 3$ , 所以不等式 $\left( {x - 2}\right) f\left( x\right)  < 0$ 的解集为 $\left( {0,2}\right)  \cup  \left( {3, + \infty }\right)$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知定义在 $\left\lbrack  {-3,3}\right\rbrack$ 上的函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{-x} - {2x} + 1$ ，若 $f\left( {m}^{2}\right)  +  f\left( {m - 2}\right)  \leq  2$ ，则 $m$ 的取值范围是\blkda{$\left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$}

        \begin{jieda}
            记 $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{-x} - {2x},x \in  \left\lbrack  {-3,3}\right\rbrack$ ,则 $g\left( {-x}\right)  = {{e}}^{-x} - {{e}}^{x} + {2x} =  - g\left( x\right)$，故 $g\left( x\right)$ 为 $\left\lbrack  {-3,3}\right\rbrack$ 的奇函数
            
            因为${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + {{e}}^{-x} - 2 \geq  2\sqrt{{{e}}^{x}{{e}}^{-x}} - 2 = 0$，所以$g\left( x\right)$ 为 $\left\lbrack  {-3,3}\right\rbrack$ 上的单调递增函数
            
            因为 $f\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)  + 1$，由 $f\left( {m}^{2}\right)  + f\left( {m - 2}\right)  \leq  2$ 可得 $g\left( {m}^{2}\right)  + g\left( {m - 2}\right)  + 2 \leq  2$ ,进而 $g\left( {m}^{2}\right)  \leq  g\left( {2 - m}\right)$ ,
            
            故 $- 3 \leq  {m}^{2} \leq  2 - m \leq  3$ ，解得 $- 1 \leq  m \leq  1$
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的函数 $f\left( x\right) ,f\left( {x - 1}\right)$ 关于直线 $x = 1$ 对称,当 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,设 $a = f\left( {\sqrt{3}\ln 2}\right) ,b = f\left( {\ln 3}\right) ,c = f\left( {-\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,则 $a,b,c$ 的大小关系为\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        \xx{$c < a < b$}{$b < a < c$}{$a > b > c$}{$a < b < c$}

        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $f\left( {x - 1}\right)$ 关于直线 $x = 1$ 对称,则 $f\left( x\right)$ 是偶函数,
            
            当 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,所以函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递增.
            
            因为 $\ln 3 > \ln {e} = 1,\left| {-\dfrac{1}{2}}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2} < 1$ ,
            
            再比较 $\sqrt{3}\ln 2$ 和 $\ln 3$ 的大小，构造函数 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\ln x}{x}$ ，
            
            则 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1 - \ln x}{{x}^{2}}$ ,令 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1 - \ln x}{{x}^{2}} > 0$ ,得 $0 < x < {e}$ ,此时 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\ln x}{x}$ 单调递增,
            
            所以 $g\left( 2\right)  = \dfrac{\ln 2}{2} > g\left( \sqrt{3}\right)  = \dfrac{\ln \sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{3}}$ ,也即 $\sqrt{3}\ln 2 > \ln 3 > \ln {e} = 1$ ,
            
            结合函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递增及偶函数可知 $a > b > c$ .
        \end{jieda}
        
        \item 已知 $a = \pi \ln 3,b = 3\ln \pi ,c = \dfrac{3\pi }{{e}}$ ，则\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        \xx{$a < b < c$}{$c < a < b$}{$b < a < c$}{$c < b < a$}
        \begin{jieda}
            因为 $a = \pi \ln 3,b = 3\ln \pi ,c = \dfrac{3\pi }{{e}}$ ,即 $\dfrac{a}{3\pi } = \dfrac{\ln 3}{3},\dfrac{b}{3\pi } = \dfrac{\ln \pi }{\pi },\dfrac{c}{3\pi } = \dfrac{1}{{e}} = \dfrac{\ln {e}}{{e}}$ ,
            
            根据特点设函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\ln x}{x},x > 0$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1 - \ln x}{{x}^{2}}$ ,
            
            当 $x \in  \left( {{e}, + \infty }\right)$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ，可知 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {{e}, + \infty }\right)$ 内单调递减，
            
            且 ${e} < 3 < \pi$ ，则 $f\left( {e}\right)  > f\left( 3\right)  > f\left( \pi \right)$ ，即 $\dfrac{c}{3\pi } > \dfrac{a}{3\pi } > \dfrac{b}{3\pi }$ ，所以 $b < a < c$ .
        \end{jieda}
    \end{enumerate}

    \item 利用导数求函数的极值
    \begin{enumerate}
        \item 已知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{2x} - 1}{x - 1}{{e}}^{x}$ ,则\dotfill{(\kh{C})}
        \xx{$f\left( x\right)$ 有极大值，无极小值}{$f\left( x\right)$ 无极大值，有极小值}{$f\left( x\right)$ 既有极大值，也有极小值}{$f\left( x\right)$ 既无极大值，也无极小值}

        \begin{jieda}
            $f\left( x\right)$ 定义域为 $\left( {-\infty ,1}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ ,且 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x\left( {{2x} - 3}\right) }{{\left( x - 1\right) }^{2}}{{e}}^{x}$ ,
            
            由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ 可得: $x \in  \left( {-\infty ,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {\dfrac{3}{2}, + \infty }\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right) ,\left( {\dfrac{3}{2}, + \infty }\right)$ 单调递增;
